- **pi**: Delegates to the pi CLI in an isolated pi session. Use for work you want done by pi's model and tools rather than Claude's - a second opinion from another provider, or a cheap bulk pass. Runs pi's own read/bash/edit/write tools, so it can change files. To choose pi's model, make the prompt's first line `pi-model: &lt;pattern&gt;` (e.g. `pi-model: openrouter/moonshotai/kimi-k2`); the pattern is anything `pi --model` accepts, `pi --list-models &lt;search&gt;` lists them, and the provider/id form avoids ambiguity. Without that line pi uses its own default model. - **pi**: Install Rapid-MLX on an Apple Silicon Mac with `curl -fsSL https://rapidmlx.com/install.sh | bash` and restart the terminal (or run `export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"`), then install Pi. Run `rapid-mlx launch pi`. The same command works after a restart; `rapid-mlx launch pi --remove` deletes only Rapid's private profile. Existing Pi settings are left untouched. - **pi-reviewer**: Use this subagent to run an adversarial review of a task's worktree. Default review gate after tester PASS; can also be invoked manually via /pi:review. ([yanyaoer/pi-agent-cc](https://github.com/yanyaoer/pi-agent-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/pi-reviewer.md))
- **pi-reviser**: Persona for the pi worker in REVISE mode (work-issue-pidev.yml, issue 2446): apply ONE owner comment to an open PR. Rendered by scripts/pipeline-prompt.mjs with --var-file feedback=&lt;file&gt;. ([FriendlyInternet/nuxt-crouton](https://github.com/FriendlyInternet/nuxt-crouton/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pi-reviser.md) · ★10)

- **pi-runner**: Hands one task to pi (`pi --mode rpc`, GLM 5.3 Flash by default, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash on request — по умолчанию; модели — из Listik) through `pi-run.sh` and returns its output verbatim — or collects the answer of an already running background job by its job-id, or continues a named session. Use when the work has been decided to go out to pi: codebase investigation, an independent read, a mechanical change from a description. Investigates and fixes nothing itself. Several tasks — launch one copy per task in a single message. ([dmitry-fomin/listik](https://github.com/dmitry-fomin/listik/blob/HEAD/plugins/pi/agents/pi-runner.md) · ★5)
- **pi-runner**: Relay that runs one headless pi job (Kimi K3 by default; any pi provider/model) and returns its stdout verbatim. A cheap fan-out worker for Workflow scripts (agentType 'pi-relay:pi-runner') or the Agent tool. It does not do the task itself. ([jakelevirne/pi-relay](https://github.com/jakelevirne/pi-relay/blob/HEAD/agents/pi-runner.md))
- **pi-sdk**: The installed Pi runtime is the source of truth for extension declarations. The npm SDK in `.pi/agent/node_modules` can lag behind the launcher. - **pi-setup-survey**: A capture of pi-coding-agent setup patterns surveyed from the upstream `earendil-works/pi-mono` dogfood config, the `badlogic/pi-skills` ecosystem, the `anthropics/skills` collection, and pi's own docs — mapped against **this repo's current pi setup**. - **pi-shim**: Transports a single shell command invoking ~/.claude/scripts/pi-shim.sh and returns stdout verbatim. - **pi-smoke**: `./input.json` names one `message` string. Read it and write `./result.json` echoing it back unchanged. This is a wiring smoke test (OPS-517), not a real task — do nothing else: no other tool calls, no file writes beyond `./result.json`. ([watt-mind/factory](https://github.com/watt-mind/factory/blob/HEAD/event-runtime/agents/pi-smoke.md) · ★20)
- **pi-staff-reviewer**: Staff/principal-level pi specialist for DELIBERATE, on-demand review of the entire local setup - settings, system append, agents, skills, extensions, packages, MCP, sandbox derivation, trust, project convergence, and session evidence. Refreshes from installed pi sources, then returns prioritized, dependency-aware recommendations, adoption opportunities, and evidence-backed artifact proposals. Invoke explicitly through setup-review under pi. Do NOT auto-delegate during normal coding tasks. Use proactively when pidex-planner produces a plan that touches core architecture, when technical approach needs validation, or for periodic health audits. - **pidex-critic**: Program-manager-style reviewer in the pidex-* pipeline that stress-tests planning documents BEFORE implementation. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-planner produces a plan. After approval, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-implementer. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-critic.md) · ★2)

- **pipe-backend**: Use when adding or changing a server endpoint, service, background job or business rule, when asked to 'build the API for X', or to fix a server-side bug inside backend-owned files.
